>>     re.I,

>>
>> def decode_uuidv7_utc(u: str) -> str:
>>     ms = int(u.replace("-", "")[:12], 16)
>>     return datetime.fromtimestamp(ms / 1000.0, tz=timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S.%f")[:-3] + " UTC"
>>
>> def scan_conversations(convs, source_label):
>>     print(f"\n=== Auditing {len(convs)} conversations from {source_label} ===")
>>     hits = 0
>>     for conv in convs:
>>         if not isinstance(conv, dict):
>>             continue
>>         title = conv.get("title") or "Untitled"
>>         cid = conv.get("id") or conv.get("conversation_id")
>>         raw = json.dumps(conv)
>>         composites = sorted(set(m.group(0) for m in UUID7_COMPOSITE_RE.finditer(raw)))
>>
>>         py_runs = []
>>         for nid, node in (conv.get("mapping") or {}).items():
>>             msg = (node or {}).get("message") or {}
>>             author = (msg.get("author") or {}).get("name") or ""
>>             agg = (msg.get("metadata") or {}).get("aggregate_result") or {}
>>             if author == "python" or agg:
>>                 py_runs.append((nid, agg.get("kernel_id"), agg.get("run_id"), agg.get("status")))
>>
>>         if composites or py_runs:
>>             hits += 1
>>             print(f"\n[{title}] (id={cid})")
>>             for comp in composites:
>>                 base_id = comp.split(":")[0]
>>                 print(f"  Composite : {comp}  [Base created: {decode_uuidv7_utc(base_id)}]")
>>             for nid, kid, rid, st in py_runs:
>>                 print(f"  PythonRun : node={nid} | kernel={kid} | run={rid} | status={st}")
>>     print(f"\nDone. Conversations with sandbox telemetry: {hits}/{len(convs)}")
>>
>> home = Path.home()
>> search_dirs = [Path.cwd(), home / "Downloads", home / "Desktop"]
>> found_any = False
>> seen = set()
>>
>> for d in search_dirs:
>>     if not d.exists():
>>         continue
>>
>>     for jf in sorted(d.glob("conversations*.json")):
>>         p = jf.resolve()
>>         if p in seen:
>>             continue
>>         seen.add(p)
>>         try:
>>             data = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
>>             if isinstance(data, list):
>>                 found_any = True
>>                 scan_conversations(data, str(p))
>>         except Exception as e:
>>             print(f"Skipped {p}: {e}")
>>
>>     for zf in sorted(d.glob("*.zip")):
>>         p = zf.resolve()
>>         if p in seen:
>>             continue
>>         seen.add(p)
>>         try:
>>             with zipfile.ZipFile(p, "r") as z:
>>                 members = [n for n in z.namelist() if re.search(r"(^|/)conversations(-\d+)?\.json$", n)]
>>                 for m in members:
>>                     data = json.loads(z.read(m).decode("utf-8"))
>>                     if isinstance(data, list):
>>                         found_any = True
>>                         scan_conversations(data, f"{p} :: {m}")
>>         except Exception:
>>             pass
>>
>>     for hf in sorted(d.glob("*.har")):
>>         p = hf.resolve()
>>         if p in seen:
>>             continue
>>         seen.add(p)
>>         raw = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
>>         matches = sorted(set(m.group(0) for m in UUID7_COMPOSITE_RE.finditer(raw)))
>>         found_any = True
>>         print(f"\n=== Auditing DevTools HAR capture: {p} ===")
>>         if matches:
>>             for comp in matches:
>>                 base_id = comp.split(":")[0]
>>                 print(f"  Composite : {comp}  [Base created: {decode_uuidv7_utc(base_id)}]")
>>         else:
>>             print("  No sandbox composite IDs found in this .har file.")
>>
>> if not found_any:
>>     print("No ChatGPT conversations.json, export .zip, or DevTools .har found yet in:")

PS C:\Users\Andrew> Add-Type -TypeDefinition @"
>> using System;
>> using System.IO;
>> using System.Text;
>> using System.Collections.Generic;
>>
>> public static class RawDiskScanner {
>>     public static List<string> Scan(string[] roots, string[] targets) {
>>         var hits = new List<string>();
>>         var subdirs = new string[] { "Local Storage", "Session Storage", "IndexedDB", "Cache", "Code Cache", "Network", "OpenAI", "ChatGPT", "Codex" };
>>
>>         var patterns = new List<Tuple<string, string, byte[]>>();
>>         foreach (var t in targets) {
>>             patterns.Add(Tuple.Create(t, "UTF-8", Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(t)));
>>             patterns.Add(Tuple.Create(t, "UTF-16LE", Encoding.Unicode.GetBytes(t)));
>>         }
>>
>>         int scanned = 0;
>>         foreach (var root in roots) {
>>             if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(root) || !Directory.Exists(root)) continue;
>>             foreach (var file in SafeEnumerateFiles(root)) {
>>                 bool matchDir = false;
>>                 foreach (var sd in subdirs) {
>>                     if (file.IndexOf(sd, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0) { matchDir = true; break; }
>>                 }
>>                 if (!matchDir) continue;
>>
>>                 try {
>>                     var fi = new FileInfo(file);
>>                     if (fi.Length == 0 || fi.Length > 100 * 1024 * 1024) continue;
>>                     scanned++;
>>
>>                     byte[] data;
>>                     using (var fs = new FileStream(file, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.ReadWrite | FileShare.Delete)) {
>>                         data = new byte[fs.Length];
>>                         int read = 0;
>>                         while (read < data.Length) {
>>                             int r = fs.Read(data, read, data.Length - read);
>>                             if (r == 0) break;
>>                             read += r;
>>                         }
>>                     }
>>
>>                     foreach (var pat in patterns) {
>>                         int idx = IndexOfBytes(data, pat.Item3, 0);
>>                         while (idx >= 0) {
>>                             int start = Math.Max(0, idx - 600);
>>                             int len = Math.Min(data.Length - start, 2400);
>>                             string ctx = ExtractPrintable(data, start, len, pat.Item2 == "UTF-16LE");
>>                             hits.Add(string.Format("[HIT] Enc={0} | ID={1}\r\nFile: {2}\r\nContext:\r\n{3}\r\n{4}",
>>                                 pat.Item2, pat.Item1, file, ctx, new string('-', 72)));
>>                             if (hits.Count >= 50) return hits;
>>                             idx = IndexOfBytes(data, pat.Item3, idx + pat.Item3.Length);
>>                         }
>>                     }
>>                 } catch { }
>>             }
>>         }
>>         hits.Insert(0, string.Format("=== Scanned {0} open/locked browser & app files | Hits: {1} ===", scanned, hits.Count));
>>         return hits;
>>     }
>>
>>     private static IEnumerable<string> SafeEnumerateFiles(string path) {
>>         var dirs = new Stack<string>();
>>         dirs.Push(path);
>>         while (dirs.Count > 0) {
>>             string current = dirs.Pop();
>>             string[] files = null;
>>             try { files = Directory.GetFiles(current); } catch { }
>>             if (files != null) { foreach (var f in files) yield return f; }
>>             string[] sub = null;
>>             try { sub = Directory.GetDirectories(current); } catch { }
>>             if (sub != null) { foreach (var d in sub) dirs.Push(d); }
>>         }
>>     }
>>
>>     private static int IndexOfBytes(byte[] hay, byte[] needle, int start) {
>>         int limit = hay.Length - needle.Length;
>>         for (int i = start; i <= limit; i++) {
>>             if (hay[i] != needle[0]) continue;
>>             bool ok = true;
>>             for (int j = 1; j < needle.Length; j++) {
>>                 if (hay[i + j] != needle[j]) { ok = false; break; }
>>             }
>>             if (ok) return i;
>>         }
>>         return -1;
>>     }
>>
>>     private static string ExtractPrintable(byte[] buf, int start, int len, bool utf16) {
>>         string raw = utf16 ? Encoding.Unicode.GetString(buf, start, len - (len % 2)) : Encoding.UTF8.GetString(buf, start, len);
>>         var sb = new StringBuilder();
>>         bool lastWasSpace = false;
>>         foreach (char c in raw) {
>>             if ((c >= 32 && c <= 126) || c == '\r' || c == '\n' || c == '\t') {
>>                 sb.Append(c);
>>                 lastWasSpace = false;
>>             } else if (!lastWasSpace) {
>>                 sb.Append(' ');
>>                 lastWasSpace = true;
>>             }
>>         }
>>         return sb.ToString();
>>     }
>> }
>> "@ -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
